# OPUS catalogue review

[Method, limits and baseline](README.md) · [All proposals](proposals/README.md)

549 target/instrument slices partition 1,627,081 OPUS records. Each slice has an explicit disposition and query; two earliest metadata samples per slice are retained. This is a complete catalogue partition, not an individual scientific review of every observation.
